## Output style

The reader has ADHD. Shape every response so it can be acted on:

1. Lead with the answer or next action: command, path, or snippet first.
2. Number multi-step work; one bounded action per step.
3. End with one next action doable in under two minutes.
4. Finish the current issue before raising a new one.
5. Restate progress each turn ("step 3 of 5 done").
6. Give time estimates in concrete units, never "a bit".
7. After a change, show what now works.
8. Errors: state location, cause, and fix. No drama.
9. Cap lists at 5 items.
10. No preamble, no recaps, no closers.

Exceptions: explain fully when asked to explain. Confirm before destructive actions. After three failed fixes, stop and name the doubtful assumption. If the request is ambiguous, ask one short question.

模块放置：

- `src/alphamill/experiment_store/identity.py`：规范化语义上下文、计算 ID、校验 supersedes；
- `src/alphamill/experiment_store/population.py`：canonical claim、registration event 与可重建索引；
- `src/alphamill/experiment_store/synthesis.py`：只读 canonical 证据并生成综合报告；
- `src/alphamill/validation/methodology_gate.py`：能力清单、静态检查与运行时边界检查；
- `src/alphamill/factor_factory/bench/`：信号、统计、成本、稳定性与 artifact schema；
- `src/alphamill/evaluation/cli.py`：薄入口，不承载裁决逻辑。

依赖方向固定为 CLI → orchestration → domain evaluators/store ports → F002 reader/filesystem。
bench 不读取环境变量、不决定 tier/窗口/cohort，也不写 official population。synthesis 不访问 preview。

## 3. 数据模型与 Migration
├── preview/<experiment_id>/<attempt_id>/{manifest.json,report.json,curves.parquet,events.jsonl}
├── bench/<object_id>/<data_version>/<experiment_id>/{manifest.json,report.json,curves.parquet,events.jsonl,registration.json}
└── cohorts/<cohort_id>/
    ├── cohort.json
    ├── events/<event_id>.json
    └── synthesis/<synthesis_id>/synthesis_report.json
```

`cohort.json` 在首个 canonical 运行前冻结，内容含 hypothesis family、全部候选承诺/纳入规则、
选择阶段、窗口、阈值和方法版本。每个承诺成员无论 PASS、FAIL、UNDERPOWERED 或 INCOMPLET<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1683" />新运行生成器；`cost_model_version` 与 `data_version` 并列进入
experiment manifest（FR7.1）。

**曲线级时间序列侧车（ADR-0005 呈现契约）**：评测台除标量结论外必须持久化曲线级时间序列，
供研究控制台渲染（以 F007 spec 为准，先于 F005 开发锁定）：

- 路径：与评测报告同目录写 `curves.parquet`
  （`reports/bench/<object_id>/<data_version>/curves.parquet`，报告根 `reports/` 见 §三 目录树），
  与 `report.json` 在同一原子批次发布；
- manifest 关联键：experiment manifest 增 `curves: {path, rows, columns, sha256}`，与报告、
  `data_version`、`cost_model_version` 并列，保证曲线可从真相源确定性重放；
- 最小列集：`equity_after_cost`（成本后累计权益）、`drawdown`（回撤）、
  `q1_cum`..`q5_cum`、`long_short_cum`（分位数/多空累计收益，按 bar 时间展开）、
  `rolling_ic_h1/h2/h4/h12/h24`（各 horizon 的滚动 IC）；时间轴为逐日/逐 bar UTC 时间戳。
  `report.json` 的 `quantile_returns`/`ic_decay` 是全样本标量汇总，侧车是其时间展开，
  两者口径必须可互推（如侧车末日累计值对齐标量分组收益）。

**综合报告契约**：F007 从 canonical experiment ledger 与曲线侧车生成不可变
`synthesis_report.json`，至少包含 cohort 漏斗、有效独立数、信号质量/组合转换/成本容量/
时序稳定/执行实现五阶段损失、最大约束，以及分栏的事实/推断/建议。它是证据与治理产物；
F005 不重新计算口径，只渲染该报告。

#### 4.2.1 组合定义与组合门

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PortfolioDef:
    portfolio_id: str
    members: list[str]             # FactorDef 或 meta-factor ID
    weights: dict[str, float]
    estimation_window: tuple[str, str]
    constraints: dict              # 换手、净敞口、容量、最小订单等
    construction_version: str
```

---
name: ci-verify
description: Run local lint/test checks before committing, then confirm CI is fully green after pushing — never report a task done with CI unchecked or red
---

## CI Verify

A task involving a git commit is not "done" when `git push` succeeds. It is
done when CI has actually finished and every job is green. Local checks
passing does not guarantee CI passes — different environment, different
dependency resolution, different tool versions.

### Before committing

1. Find the project's local check commands. Look in order: a project
   `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md`, `package.json` scripts, `Makefile`, or the CI
   workflow file itself (`.github/workflows/*.yml`, etc.) — the CI config is
   the ground truth for what "passing" means.
2. Run those commands locally (lint, format check, type check, test suite —
   whatever the project defines). Fix everything before committing; don't
   push speculatively and let CI find the problems.
3. If a check tool is only loosely version-pinned in the project (e.g.
   `ruff>=0.6` with no upper bound), be aware the CI-installed version can
   differ from the locally-installed one and silently disagree — see the
   diagnostic note below.

### After pushing

1. Get the run for this push: `gh run list --limit 1` (or filter by branch/
   workflow if there could be ambiguity with concurrent pushes).
2. Watch it to completion: `gh run watch <run-id> --exit-status`. Do not
   poll manually or guess — wait for the real result.
3. Confirm **every** job in the run succeeded, not just the one you expect
   to be affected by your change (e.g. a docs-only change can still break a
   job you didn't think to check).
4. If any job is red, treat the task as still in progress: read the failure
   log (`gh run view <run-id> --log-failed`), fix the root cause, commit,
   push, and repeat step 1–3. Only report the task complete once a run
   triggered by the current HEAD is fully green.

### Diagnostic tip: "local green, CI red, and the failure is unrelated to my diff"

This pattern usually means environment/dependency drift, not a bug in the
change just made. Before searching further:

- Compare the locally installed version of the failing tool against what
  the project's dependency file would resolve to fresh (e.g. `ruff --version`
  locally vs. the version range in `pyproject.toml`/`package.json`). CI
  typically does a clean install every run and will pick up new releases of
  loosely-pinned dev dependencies that a local environment — often installed
  once, months ago — never picked up.
- If that's the cause, the durable fix is to pin the dependency to a bounded
  range (not just a floor) in the project's manifest, verify locally with
  that exact version, and only then commit the pin change alongside any
  config adjustments the new version requires.

### Environment note: `gh` unavailable in WSL

If `gh` is not found in your shell but works on Windows (e.g. WSL with the
Windows GitHub CLI installed), don't treat it as a missing credential and
stop to ask the user. Create a symlink into a PATH directory — the Windows
keyring auth is shared, no re-login needed:

```bash
ln -s "/mnt/c/Program Files/GitHub CLI/gh.exe" /mnt/c/Users/<you>/.local/bin/gh
```

Verify once with `gh run list --limit 1`. If the Windows GitHub CLI is not
installed at all, then (and only then) credential provisioning is a real
user-gated step — surface it once with the exact install command instead of
re-prompting per command.

name: CI

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
  pull_request:

# 同一分支的新提交取消仍在跑的旧任务，避免排队浪费。
concurrency:
  group: ${{ github.workflow }}-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

# 供应链加固：本工作流只读仓库，不需要写权限。
permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  # 步骤清单的唯一真源是 tools/verify.py：CI 只调用它，不复制各步骤
  # （生命周期 / 文档链接 / 依赖 pin / pytest / ruff 全部由 verify.py 提供）。
  verify:
    name: verify (py${{ matrix.python-version }})
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        # 下限锁 requires-python；上限跟随，用于尽早发现新版本行为差异。
        python-version: ["3.11", "3.13"]
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v7
        with:
          python-version: ${{ matrix.python-version }}
      - run: pip install -e ".[dev]"
      - name: 统一验证（verify.py 全部步骤）
        run: python3 tools/verify.py
CLAUDE.md:34:- 测试/质量：pytest + ruff（check + format），唯一入口 `python3 tools/verify.py`
CLAUDE.md:42:- 质量门禁：统一入口 `python3 tools/verify.py`（串联文档门禁与测试；技术栈落地后扩展 lint/
.github/workflows/ci.yml:18:  # 步骤清单的唯一真源是 tools/verify.py：CI 只调用它，不复制各步骤
.github/workflows/ci.yml:35:        run: python3 tools/verify.py

模块放置：

- `src/alphamill/experiment_store/identity.py`：规范化语义上下文、计算 ID、校验 supersedes；
- `src/alphamill/experiment_store/population.py`：canonical claim、registration event 与可重建索引；
- `src/alphamill/experiment_store/synthesis.py`：只读 canonical 证据并生成综合报告；
- `src/alphamill/validation/methodology_gate.py`：能力清单、静态检查与运行时边界检查；
- `src/alphamill/factor_factory/bench/`：信号、统计、成本、稳定性与 artifact schema；
- `src/alphamill/evaluation/cli.py`：薄入口，不承载裁决逻辑。

依赖方向固定为 CLI → orchestration → domain evaluators/store ports → F002 reader/filesystem。
bench 不读取环境变量、不决定 tier/窗口/cohort，也不写 official population。synthesis 不访问 preview。

## 3. 数据模型与 Migration
├── preview/<experiment_id>/<attempt_id>/{manifest.json,report.json,curves.parquet,events.jsonl}
├── bench/<object_id>/<data_version>/<experiment_id>/{manifest.json,report.json,curves.parquet,events.jsonl,registration.json}
└── cohorts/<cohort_id>/
    ├── cohort.json
    ├── events/<event_id>.json
    └── synthesis/<synthesis_id>/synthesis_report.json
```

`cohort.json` 在首个 canonical 运行前冻结，内容含 hypothesis family、全部候选承诺/纳入规则、
选择阶段、窗口、阈值和方法版本。每个承诺成员无论 PASS、FAIL、UNDERPOWERED 或 INCOMPLET<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1683" />新运行生成器；`cost_model_version` 与 `data_version` 并列进入
experiment manifest（FR7.1）。

**曲线级时间序列侧车（ADR-0005 呈现契约）**：评测台除标量结论外必须持久化曲线级时间序列，
供研究控制台渲染（以 F007 spec 为准，先于 F005 开发锁定）：

- 路径：与评测报告同目录写 `curves.parquet`
  （`reports/bench/<object_id>/<data_version>/curves.parquet`，报告根 `reports/` 见 §三 目录树），
  与 `report.json` 在同一原子批次发布；
- manifest 关联键：experiment manifest 增 `curves: {path, rows, columns, sha256}`，与报告、
  `data_version`、`cost_model_version` 并列，保证曲线可从真相源确定性重放；
- 最小列集：`equity_after_cost`（成本后累计权益）、`drawdown`（回撤）、
  `q1_cum`..`q5_cum`、`long_short_cum`（分位数/多空累计收益，按 bar 时间展开）、
  `rolling_ic_h1/h2/h4/h12/h24`（各 horizon 的滚动 IC）；时间轴为逐日/逐 bar UTC 时间戳。
  `report.json` 的 `quantile_returns`/`ic_decay` 是全样本标量汇总，侧车是其时间展开，
  两者口径必须可互推（如侧车末日累计值对齐标量分组收益）。

**综合报告契约**：F007 从 canonical experiment ledger 与曲线侧车生成不可变
`synthesis_report.json`，至少包含 cohort 漏斗、有效独立数、信号质量/组合转换/成本容量/
时序稳定/执行实现五阶段损失、最大约束，以及分栏的事实/推断/建议。它是证据与治理产物；
F005 不重新计算口径，只渲染该报告。

#### 4.2.1 组合定义与组合门

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PortfolioDef:
    portfolio_id: str
    members: list[str]             # FactorDef 或 meta-factor ID
    weights: dict[str, float]
    estimation_window: tuple[str, str]
    constraints: dict              # 换手、净敞口、容量、最小订单等
    construction_version: str
```

---
name: ci-verify
description: Run local lint/test checks before committing, then confirm CI is fully green after pushing — never report a task done with CI unchecked or red
---

## CI Verify

A task involving a git commit is not "done" when `git push` succeeds. It is
done when CI has actually finished and every job is green. Local checks
passing does not guarantee CI passes — different environment, different
dependency resolution, different tool versions.

### Before committing

1. Find the project's local check commands. Look in order: a project
   `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md`, `package.json` scripts, `Makefile`, or the CI
   workflow file itself (`.github/workflows/*.yml`, etc.) — the CI config is
   the ground truth for what "passing" means.
2. Run those commands locally (lint, format check, type check, test suite —
   whatever the project defines). Fix everything before committing; don't
   push speculatively and let CI find the problems.
3. If a check tool is only loosely version-pinned in the project (e.g.
   `ruff>=0.6` with no upper bound), be aware the CI-installed version can
   differ from the locally-installed one and silently disagree — see the
   diagnostic note below.

### After pushing

1. Get the run for this push: `gh run list --limit 1` (or filter by branch/
   workflow if there could be ambiguity with concurrent pushes).
2. Watch it to completion: `gh run watch <run-id> --exit-status`. Do not
   poll manually or guess — wait for the real result.
3. Confirm **every** job in the run succeeded, not just the one you expect
   to be affected by your change (e.g. a docs-only change can still break a
   job you didn't think to check).
4. If any job is red, treat the task as still in progress: read the failure
   log (`gh run view <run-id> --log-failed`), fix the root cause, commit,
   push, and repeat step 1–3. Only report the task complete once a run
   triggered by the current HEAD is fully green.

### Diagnostic tip: "local green, CI red, and the failure is unrelated to my diff"

This pattern usually means environment/dependency drift, not a bug in the
change just made. Before searching further:

- Compare the locally installed version of the failing tool against what
  the project's dependency file would resolve to fresh (e.g. `ruff --version`
  locally vs. the version range in `pyproject.toml`/`package.json`). CI
  typically does a clean install every run and will pick up new releases of
  loosely-pinned dev dependencies that a local environment — often installed
  once, months ago — never picked up.
- If that's the cause, the durable fix is to pin the dependency to a bounded
  range (not just a floor) in the project's manifest, verify locally with
  that exact version, and only then commit the pin change alongside any
  config adjustments the new version requires.

### Environment note: `gh` unavailable in WSL

If `gh` is not found in your shell but works on Windows (e.g. WSL with the
Windows GitHub CLI installed), don't treat it as a missing credential and
stop to ask the user. Create a symlink into a PATH directory — the Windows
keyring auth is shared, no re-login needed:

```bash
ln -s "/mnt/c/Program Files/GitHub CLI/gh.exe" /mnt/c/Users/<you>/.local/bin/gh
```

Verify once with `gh run list --limit 1`. If the Windows GitHub CLI is not
installed at all, then (and only then) credential provisioning is a real
user-gated step — surface it once with the exact install command instead of
re-prompting per command.

name: CI

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
  pull_request:

# 同一分支的新提交取消仍在跑的旧任务，避免排队浪费。
concurrency:
  group: ${{ github.workflow }}-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

# 供应链加固：本工作流只读仓库，不需要写权限。
permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  # 步骤清单的唯一真源是 tools/verify.py：CI 只调用它，不复制各步骤
  # （生命周期 / 文档链接 / 依赖 pin / pytest / ruff 全部由 verify.py 提供）。
  verify:
    name: verify (py${{ matrix.python-version }})
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        # 下限锁 requires-python；上限跟随，用于尽早发现新版本行为差异。
        python-version: ["3.11", "3.13"]
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v7
        with:
          python-version: ${{ matrix.python-version }}
      - run: pip install -e ".[dev]"
      - name: 统一验证（verify.py 全部步骤）
        run: python3 tools/verify.py
CLAUDE.md:34:- 测试/质量：pytest + ruff（check + format），唯一入口 `python3 tools/verify.py`
CLAUDE.md:42:- 质量门禁：统一入口 `python3 tools/verify.py`（串联文档门禁与测试；技术栈落地后扩展 lint/
.github/workflows/ci.yml:18:  # 步骤清单的唯一真源是 tools/verify.py：CI 只调用它，不复制各步骤
.github/workflows/ci.yml:35:        run: python3 tools/verify.py

模块放置：

- `src/alphamill/experiment_store/identity.py`：规范化语义上下文、计算 ID、校验 supersedes；
- `src/alphamill/experiment_store/population.py`：canonical claim、registration event 与可重建索引；
- `src/alphamill/experiment_store/synthesis.py`：只读 canonical 证据并生成综合报告；
- `src/alphamill/validation/methodology_gate.py`：能力清单、静态检查与运行时边界检查；
- `src/alphamill/factor_factory/bench/`：信号、统计、成本、稳定性与 artifact schema；
- `src/alphamill/evaluation/cli.py`：薄入口，不承载裁决逻辑。

依赖方向固定为 CLI → orchestration → domain evaluators/store ports → F002 reader/filesystem。
bench 不读取环境变量、不决定 tier/窗口/cohort，也不写 official population。synthesis 不访问 preview。

## 3. 数据模型与 Migration
├── preview/<experiment_id>/<attempt_id>/{manifest.json,report.json,curves.parquet,events.jsonl}
├── bench/<object_id>/<data_version>/<experiment_id>/{manifest.json,report.json,curves.parquet,events.jsonl,registration.json}
└── cohorts/<cohort_id>/
    ├── cohort.json
    ├── events/<event_id>.json
    └── synthesis/<synthesis_id>/synthesis_report.json
```

`cohort.json` 在首个 canonical 运行前冻结，内容含 hypothesis family、全部候选承诺/纳入规则、
选择阶段、窗口、阈值和方法版本。每个承诺成员无论 PASS、FAIL、UNDERPOWERED 或 INCOMPLET<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1683" />新运行生成器；`cost_model_version` 与 `data_version` 并列进入
experiment manifest（FR7.1）。

**曲线级时间序列侧车（ADR-0005 呈现契约）**：评测台除标量结论外必须持久化曲线级时间序列，
供研究控制台渲染（以 F007 spec 为准，先于 F005 开发锁定）：

- 路径：与评测报告同目录写 `curves.parquet`
  （`reports/bench/<object_id>/<data_version>/curves.parquet`，报告根 `reports/` 见 §三 目录树），
  与 `report.json` 在同一原子批次发布；
- manifest 关联键：experiment manifest 增 `curves: {path, rows, columns, sha256}`，与报告、
  `data_version`、`cost_model_version` 并列，保证曲线可从真相源确定性重放；
- 最小列集：`equity_after_cost`（成本后累计权益）、`drawdown`（回撤）、
  `q1_cum`..`q5_cum`、`long_short_cum`（分位数/多空累计收益，按 bar 时间展开）、
  `rolling_ic_h1/h2/h4/h12/h24`（各 horizon 的滚动 IC）；时间轴为逐日/逐 bar UTC 时间戳。
  `report.json` 的 `quantile_returns`/`ic_decay` 是全样本标量汇总，侧车是其时间展开，
  两者口径必须可互推（如侧车末日累计值对齐标量分组收益）。

**综合报告契约**：F007 从 canonical experiment ledger 与曲线侧车生成不可变
`synthesis_report.json`，至少包含 cohort 漏斗、有效独立数、信号质量/组合转换/成本容量/
时序稳定/执行实现五阶段损失、最大约束，以及分栏的事实/推断/建议。它是证据与治理产物；
F005 不重新计算口径，只渲染该报告。

#### 4.2.1 组合定义与组合门

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PortfolioDef:
    portfolio_id: str
    members: list[str]             # FactorDef 或 meta-factor ID
    weights: dict[str, float]
    estimation_window: tuple[str, str]
    constraints: dict              # 换手、净敞口、容量、最小订单等
    construction_version: str
```

---
name: ci-verify
description: Run local lint/test checks before committing, then confirm CI is fully green after pushing — never report a task done with CI unchecked or red
---

## CI Verify

A task involving a git commit is not "done" when `git push` succeeds. It is
done when CI has actually finished and every job is green. Local checks
passing does not guarantee CI passes — different environment, different
dependency resolution, different tool versions.

### Before committing

1. Find the project's local check commands. Look in order: a project
   `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md`, `package.json` scripts, `Makefile`, or the CI
   workflow file itself (`.github/workflows/*.yml`, etc.) — the CI config is
   the ground truth for what "passing" means.
2. Run those commands locally (lint, format check, type check, test suite —
   whatever the project defines). Fix everything before committing; don't
   push speculatively and let CI find the problems.
3. If a check tool is only loosely version-pinned in the project (e.g.
   `ruff>=0.6` with no upper bound), be aware the CI-installed version can
   differ from the locally-installed one and silently disagree — see the
   diagnostic note below.

### After pushing

1. Get the run for this push: `gh run list --limit 1` (or filter by branch/
   workflow if there could be ambiguity with concurrent pushes).
2. Watch it to completion: `gh run watch <run-id> --exit-status`. Do not
   poll manually or guess — wait for the real result.
3. Confirm **every** job in the run succeeded, not just the one you expect
   to be affected by your change (e.g. a docs-only change can still break a
   job you didn't think to check).
4. If any job is red, treat the task as still in progress: read the failure
   log (`gh run view <run-id> --log-failed`), fix the root cause, commit,
   push, and repeat step 1–3. Only report the task complete once a run
   triggered by the current HEAD is fully green.

### Diagnostic tip: "local green, CI red, and the failure is unrelated to my diff"

This pattern usually means environment/dependency drift, not a bug in the
change just made. Before searching further:

- Compare the locally installed version of the failing tool against what
  the project's dependency file would resolve to fresh (e.g. `ruff --version`
  locally vs. the version range in `pyproject.toml`/`package.json`). CI
  typically does a clean install every run and will pick up new releases of
  loosely-pinned dev dependencies that a local environment — often installed
  once, months ago — never picked up.
- If that's the cause, the durable fix is to pin the dependency to a bounded
  range (not just a floor) in the project's manifest, verify locally with
  that exact version, and only then commit the pin change alongside any
  config adjustments the new version requires.

### Environment note: `gh` unavailable in WSL

If `gh` is not found in your shell but works on Windows (e.g. WSL with the
Windows GitHub CLI installed), don't treat it as a missing credential and
stop to ask the user. Create a symlink into a PATH directory — the Windows
keyring auth is shared, no re-login needed:

```bash
ln -s "/mnt/c/Program Files/GitHub CLI/gh.exe" /mnt/c/Users/<you>/.local/bin/gh
```

Verify once with `gh run list --limit 1`. If the Windows GitHub CLI is not
installed at all, then (and only then) credential provisioning is a real
user-gated step — surface it once with the exact install command instead of
re-prompting per command.

name: CI

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
  pull_request:

# 同一分支的新提交取消仍在跑的旧任务，避免排队浪费。
concurrency:
  group: ${{ github.workflow }}-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

# 供应链加固：本工作流只读仓库，不需要写权限。
permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  # 步骤清单的唯一真源是 tools/verify.py：CI 只调用它，不复制各步骤
  # （生命周期 / 文档链接 / 依赖 pin / pytest / ruff 全部由 verify.py 提供）。
  verify:
    name: verify (py${{ matrix.python-version }})
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        # 下限锁 requires-python；上限跟随，用于尽早发现新版本行为差异。
        python-version: ["3.11", "3.13"]
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v7
        with:
          python-version: ${{ matrix.python-version }}
      - run: pip install -e ".[dev]"
      - name: 统一验证（verify.py 全部步骤）
        run: python3 tools/verify.py
CLAUDE.md:34:- 测试/质量：pytest + ruff（check + format），唯一入口 `python3 tools/verify.py`
CLAUDE.md:42:- 质量门禁：统一入口 `python3 tools/verify.py`（串联文档门禁与测试；技术栈落地后扩展 lint/
.github/workflows/ci.yml:18:  # 步骤清单的唯一真源是 tools/verify.py：CI 只调用它，不复制各步骤
.github/workflows/ci.yml:35:        run: python3 tools/verify.py

---
name: ci-verify
description: Run local lint/test checks before committing, then confirm CI is fully green after pushing — never report a task done with CI unchecked or red
---

## CI Verify

A task involving a git commit is not "done" when `git push` succeeds. It is
done when CI has actually finished and every job is green. Local checks
passing does not guarantee CI passes — different environment, different
dependency resolution, different tool versions.

### Before committing

1. Find the project's local check commands. Look in order: a project
   `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md`, `package.json` scripts, `Makefile`, or the CI
   workflow file itself (`.github/workflows/*.yml`, etc.) — the CI config is
   the ground truth for what "passing" means.
2. Run those commands locally (lint, format check, type check, test suite —
   whatever the project defines). Fix everything before committing; don't
   push speculatively and let CI find the problems.
3. If a check tool is only loosely version-pinned in the project (e.g.
   `ruff>=0.6` with no upper bound), be aware the CI-installed version can
   differ from the locally-installed one and silently disagree — see the
   diagnostic note below.

### After pushing

1. Get the run for this push: `gh run list --limit 1` (or filter by branch/
   workflow if there could be ambiguity with concurrent pushes).
2. Watch it to completion: `gh run watch <run-id> --exit-status`. Do not
   poll manually or guess — wait for the real result.
3. Confirm **every** job in the run succeeded, not just the one you expect
   to be affected by your change (e.g. a docs-only change can still break a
   job you didn't think to check).
4. If any job is red, treat the task as still in progress: read the failure
   log (`gh run view <run-id> --log-failed`), fix the root cause, commit,
   push, and repeat step 1–3. Only report the task complete once a run
   triggered by the current HEAD is fully green.

### Diagnostic tip: "local green, CI red, and the failure is unrelated to my diff"

This pattern usually means environment/dependency drift, not a bug in the
change just made. Before searching further:

- Compare the locally installed version of the failing tool against what
  the project's dependency file would resolve to fresh (e.g. `ruff --version`
  locally vs. the version range in `pyproject.toml`/`package.json`). CI
  typically does a clean install every run and will pick up new releases of
  loosely-pinned dev dependencies that a local environment — often installed
  once, months ago — never picked up.
- If that's the cause, the durable fix is to pin the dependency to a bounded
  range (not just a floor) in the project's manifest, verify locally with
  that exact version, and only then commit the pin change alongside any
  config adjustments the new version requires.

### Environment note: `gh` unavailable in WSL

If `gh` is not found in your shell but works on Windows (e.g. WSL with the
Windows GitHub CLI installed), don't treat it as a missing credential and
stop to ask the user. Create a symlink into a PATH directory — the Windows
keyring auth is shared, no re-login needed:

```bash
ln -s "/mnt/c/Program Files/GitHub CLI/gh.exe" /mnt/c/Users/<you>/.local/bin/gh
```

Verify once with `gh run list --limit 1`. If the Windows GitHub CLI is not
installed at all, then (and only then) credential provisioning is a real
user-gated step — surface it once with the exact install command instead of
re-prompting per command.

name: CI

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
  pull_request:

# 同一分支的新提交取消仍在跑的旧任务，避免排队浪费。
concurrency:
  group: ${{ github.workflow }}-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

# 供应链加固：本工作流只读仓库，不需要写权限。
permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  # 步骤清单的唯一真源是 tools/verify.py：CI 只调用它，不复制各步骤
  # （生命周期 / 文档链接 / 依赖 pin / pytest / ruff 全部由 verify.py 提供）。
  verify:
    name: verify (py${{ matrix.python-version }})
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        # 下限锁 requires-python；上限跟随，用于尽早发现新版本行为差异。
        python-version: ["3.11", "3.13"]
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v7
        with:
          python-version: ${{ matrix.python-version }}
      - run: pip install -e ".[dev]"
      - name: 统一验证（verify.py 全部步骤）
        run: python3 tools/verify.py
CLAUDE.md:34:- 测试/质量：pytest + ruff（check + format），唯一入口 `python3 tools/verify.py`
CLAUDE.md:42:- 质量门禁：统一入口 `python3 tools/verify.py`（串联文档门禁与测试；技术栈落地后扩展 lint/
.github/workflows/ci.yml:18:  # 步骤清单的唯一真源是 tools/verify.py：CI 只调用它，不复制各步骤
.github/workflows/ci.yml:35:        run: python3 tools/verify.py

---
name: ci-verify
description: Run local lint/test checks before committing, then confirm CI is fully green after pushing — never report a task done with CI unchecked or red
---

## CI Verify

A task involving a git commit is not "done" when `git push` succeeds. It is
done when CI has actually finished and every job is green. Local checks
passing does not guarantee CI passes — different environment, different
dependency resolution, different tool versions.

### Before committing

1. Find the project's local check commands. Look in order: a project
   `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md`, `package.json` scripts, `Makefile`, or the CI
   workflow file itself (`.github/workflows/*.yml`, etc.) — the CI config is
   the ground truth for what "passing" means.
2. Run those commands locally (lint, format check, type check, test suite —
   whatever the project defines). Fix everything before committing; don't
   push speculatively and let CI find the problems.
3. If a check tool is only loosely version-pinned in the project (e.g.
   `ruff>=0.6` with no upper bound), be aware the CI-installed version can
   differ from the locally-installed one and silently disagree — see the
   diagnostic note below.

### After pushing

1. Get the run for this push: `gh run list --limit 1` (or filter by branch/
   workflow if there could be ambiguity with concurrent pushes).
2. Watch it to completion: `gh run watch <run-id> --exit-status`. Do not
   poll manually or guess — wait for the real result.
3. Confirm **every** job in the run succeeded, not just the one you expect
   to be affected by your change (e.g. a docs-only change can still break a
   job you didn't think to check).
4. If any job is red, treat the task as still in progress: read the failure
   log (`gh run view <run-id> --log-failed`), fix the root cause, commit,
   push, and repeat step 1–3. Only report the task complete once a run
   triggered by the current HEAD is fully green.

### Diagnostic tip: "local green, CI red, and the failure is unrelated to my diff"

This pattern usually means environment/dependency drift, not a bug in the
change just made. Before searching further:

- Compare the locally installed version of the failing tool against what
  the project's dependency file would resolve to fresh (e.g. `ruff --version`
  locally vs. the version range in `pyproject.toml`/`package.json`). CI
  typically does a clean install every run and will pick up new releases of
  loosely-pinned dev dependencies that a local environment — often installed
  once, months ago — never picked up.
- If that's the cause, the durable fix is to pin the dependency to a bounded
  range (not just a floor) in the project's manifest, verify locally with
  that exact version, and only then commit the pin change alongside any
  config adjustments the new version requires.

### Environment note: `gh` unavailable in WSL

If `gh` is not found in your shell but works on Windows (e.g. WSL with the
Windows GitHub CLI installed), don't treat it as a missing credential and
stop to ask the user. Create a symlink into a PATH directory — the Windows
keyring auth is shared, no re-login needed:

```bash
ln -s "/mnt/c/Program Files/GitHub CLI/gh.exe" /mnt/c/Users/<you>/.local/bin/gh
```

Verify once with `gh run list --limit 1`. If the Windows GitHub CLI is not
installed at all, then (and only then) credential provisioning is a real
user-gated step — surface it once with the exact install command instead of
re-prompting per command.

name: CI

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
  pull_request:

# 同一分支的新提交取消仍在跑的旧任务，避免排队浪费。
concurrency:
  group: ${{ github.workflow }}-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

# 供应链加固：本工作流只读仓库，不需要写权限。
permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  # 步骤清单的唯一真源是 tools/verify.py：CI 只调用它，不复制各步骤
  # （生命周期 / 文档链接 / 依赖 pin / pytest / ruff 全部由 verify.py 提供）。
  verify:
    name: verify (py${{ matrix.python-version }})
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        # 下限锁 requires-python；上限跟随，用于尽早发现新版本行为差异。
        python-version: ["3.11", "3.13"]
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v7
        with:
          python-version: ${{ matrix.python-version }}
      - run: pip install -e ".[dev]"
      - name: 统一验证（verify.py 全部步骤）
        run: python3 tools/verify.py
CLAUDE.md:34:- 测试/质量：pytest + ruff（check + format），唯一入口 `python3 tools/verify.py`
CLAUDE.md:42:- 质量门禁：统一入口 `python3 tools/verify.py`（串联文档门禁与测试；技术栈落地后扩展 lint/
.github/workflows/ci.yml:18:  # 步骤清单的唯一真源是 tools/verify.py：CI 只调用它，不复制各步骤
.github/workflows/ci.yml:35:        run: python3 tools/verify.py

---
name: ci-verify
description: Run local lint/test checks before committing, then confirm CI is fully green after pushing — never report a task done with CI unchecked or red
---

## CI Verify

A task involving a git commit is not "done" when `git push` succeeds. It is
done when CI has actually finished and every job is green. Local checks
passing does not guarantee CI passes — different environment, different
dependency resolution, different tool versions.

### Before committing

1. Find the project's local check commands. Look in order: a project
   `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md`, `package.json` scripts, `Makefile`, or the CI
   workflow file itself (`.github/workflows/*.yml`, etc.) — the CI config is
   the ground truth for what "passing" means.
2. Run those commands locally (lint, format check, type check, test suite —
   whatever the project defines). Fix everything before committing; don't
   push speculatively and let CI find the problems.
3. If a check tool is only loosely version-pinned in the project (e.g.
   `ruff>=0.6` with no upper bound), be aware the CI-installed version can
   differ from the locally-installed one and silently disagree — see the
   diagnostic note below.

### After pushing

1. Get the run for this push: `gh run list --limit 1` (or filter by branch/
   workflow if there could be ambiguity with concurrent pushes).
2. Watch it to completion: `gh run watch <run-id> --exit-status`. Do not
   poll manually or guess — wait for the real result.
3. Confirm **every** job in the run succeeded, not just the one you expect
   to be affected by your change (e.g. a docs-only change can still break a
   job you didn't think to check).
4. If any job is red, treat the task as still in progress: read the failure
   log (`gh run view <run-id> --log-failed`), fix the root cause, commit,
   push, and repeat step 1–3. Only report the task complete once a run
   triggered by the current HEAD is fully green.

### Diagnostic tip: "local green, CI red, and the failure is unrelated to my diff"

This pattern usually means environment/dependency drift, not a bug in the
change just made. Before searching further:

- Compare the locally installed version of the failing tool against what
  the project's dependency file would resolve to fresh (e.g. `ruff --version`
  locally vs. the version range in `pyproject.toml`/`package.json`). CI
  typically does a clean install every run and will pick up new releases of
  loosely-pinned dev dependencies that a local environment — often installed
  once, months ago — never picked up.
- If that's the cause, the durable fix is to pin the dependency to a bounded
  range (not just a floor) in the project's manifest, verify locally with
  that exact version, and only then commit the pin change alongside any
  config adjustments the new version requires.

### Environment note: `gh` unavailable in WSL

If `gh` is not found in your shell but works on Windows (e.g. WSL with the
Windows GitHub CLI installed), don't treat it as a missing credential and
stop to ask the user. Create a symlink into a PATH directory — the Windows
keyring auth is shared, no re-login needed:

```bash
ln -s "/mnt/c/Program Files/GitHub CLI/gh.exe" /mnt/c/Users/<you>/.local/bin/gh
```

Verify once with `gh run list --limit 1`. If the Windows GitHub CLI is not
installed at all, then (and only then) credential provisioning is a real
user-gated step — surface it once with the exact install command instead of
re-prompting per command.

name: CI

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
  pull_request:

# 同一分支的新提交取消仍在跑的旧任务，避免排队浪费。
concurrency:
  group: ${{ github.workflow }}-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

# 供应链加固：本工作流只读仓库，不需要写权限。
permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  # 步骤清单的唯一真源是 tools/verify.py：CI 只调用它，不复制各步骤
  # （生命周期 / 文档链接 / 依赖 pin / pytest / ruff 全部由 verify.py 提供）。
  verify:
    name: verify (py${{ matrix.python-version }})
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        # 下限锁 requires-python；上限跟随，用于尽早发现新版本行为差异。
        python-version: ["3.11", "3.13"]
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v7
        with:
          python-version: ${{ matrix.python-version }}
      - run: pip install -e ".[dev]"
      - name: 统一验证（verify.py 全部步骤）
        run: python3 tools/verify.py
CLAUDE.md:34:- 测试/质量：pytest + ruff（check + format），唯一入口 `python3 tools/verify.py`
CLAUDE.md:42:- 质量门禁：统一入口 `python3 tools/verify.py`（串联文档门禁与测试；技术栈落地后扩展 lint/
.github/workflows/ci.yml:18:  # 步骤清单的唯一真源是 tools/verify.py：CI 只调用它，不复制各步骤
.github/workflows/ci.yml:35:        run: python3 tools/verify.py

---
name: ci-verify
description: Run local lint/test checks before committing, then confirm CI is fully green after pushing — never report a task done with CI unchecked or red
---

## CI Verify

A task involving a git commit is not "done" when `git push` succeeds. It is
done when CI has actually finished and every job is green. Local checks
passing does not guarantee CI passes — different environment, different
dependency resolution, different tool versions.

### Before committing

1. Find the project's local check commands. Look in order: a project
   `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md`, `package.json` scripts, `Makefile`, or the CI
   workflow file itself (`.github/workflows/*.yml`, etc.) — the CI config is
   the ground truth for what "passing" means.
2. Run those commands locally (lint, format check, type check, test suite —
   whatever the project defines). Fix everything before committing; don't
   push speculatively and let CI find the problems.
3. If a check tool is only loosely version-pinned in the project (e.g.
   `ruff>=0.6` with no upper bound), be aware the CI-installed version can
   differ from the locally-installed one and silently disagree — see the
   diagnostic note below.

### After pushing

1. Get the run for this push: `gh run list --limit 1` (or filter by branch/
   workflow if there could be ambiguity with concurrent pushes).
2. Watch it to completion: `gh run watch <run-id> --exit-status`. Do not
   poll manually or guess — wait for the real result.
3. Confirm **every** job in the run succeeded, not just the one you expect
   to be affected by your change (e.g. a docs-only change can still break a
   job you didn't think to check).
4. If any job is red, treat the task as still in progress: read the failure
   log (`gh run view <run-id> --log-failed`), fix the root cause, commit,
   push, and repeat step 1–3. Only report the task complete once a run
   triggered by the current HEAD is fully green.

### Diagnostic tip: "local green, CI red, and the failure is unrelated to my diff"

This pattern usually means environment/dependency drift, not a bug in the
change just made. Before searching further:

- Compare the locally installed version of the failing tool against what
  the project's dependency file would resolve to fresh (e.g. `ruff --version`
  locally vs. the version range in `pyproject.toml`/`package.json`). CI
  typically does a clean install every run and will pick up new releases of
  loosely-pinned dev dependencies that a local environment — often installed
  once, months ago — never picked up.
- If that's the cause, the durable fix is to pin the dependency to a bounded
  range (not just a floor) in the project's manifest, verify locally with
  that exact version, and only then commit the pin change alongside any
  config adjustments the new version requires.

### Environment note: `gh` unavailable in WSL

If `gh` is not found in your shell but works on Windows (e.g. WSL with the
Windows GitHub CLI installed), don't treat it as a missing credential and
stop to ask the user. Create a symlink into a PATH directory — the Windows
keyring auth is shared, no re-login needed:

```bash
ln -s "/mnt/c/Program Files/GitHub CLI/gh.exe" /mnt/c/Users/<you>/.local/bin/gh
```

Verify once with `gh run list --limit 1`. If the Windows GitHub CLI is not
installed at all, then (and only then) credential provisioning is a real
user-gated step — surface it once with the exact install command instead of
re-prompting per command.

name: CI

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
  pull_request:

# 同一分支的新提交取消仍在跑的旧任务，避免排队浪费。
concurrency:
  group: ${{ github.workflow }}-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

# 供应链加固：本工作流只读仓库，不需要写权限。
permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  # 步骤清单的唯一真源是 tools/verify.py：CI 只调用它，不复制各步骤
  # （生命周期 / 文档链接 / 依赖 pin / pytest / ruff 全部由 verify.py 提供）。
  verify:
    name: verify (py${{ matrix.python-version }})
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        # 下限锁 requires-python；上限跟随，用于尽早发现新版本行为差异。
        python-version: ["3.11", "3.13"]
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v7
        with:
          python-version: ${{ matrix.python-version }}
      - run: pip install -e ".[dev]"
      - name: 统一验证（verify.py 全部步骤）
        run: python3 tools/verify.py
CLAUDE.md:34:- 测试/质量：pytest + ruff（check + format），唯一入口 `python3 tools/verify.py`
CLAUDE.md:42:- 质量门禁：统一入口 `python3 tools/verify.py`（串联文档门禁与测试；技术栈落地后扩展 lint/
.github/workflows/ci.yml:18:  # 步骤清单的唯一真源是 tools/verify.py：CI 只调用它，不复制各步骤
.github/workflows/ci.yml:35:        run: python3 tools/verify.py

---
name: ci-verify
description: Run local lint/test checks before committing, then confirm CI is fully green after pushing — never report a task done with CI unchecked or red
---

## CI Verify

A task involving a git commit is not "done" when `git push` succeeds. It is
done when CI has actually finished and every job is green. Local checks
passing does not guarantee CI passes — different environment, different
dependency resolution, different tool versions.

### Before committing

1. Find the project's local check commands. Look in order: a project
   `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md`, `package.json` scripts, `Makefile`, or the CI
   workflow file itself (`.github/workflows/*.yml`, etc.) — the CI config is
   the ground truth for what "passing" means.
2. Run those commands locally (lint, format check, type check, test suite —
   whatever the project defines). Fix everything before committing; don't
   push speculatively and let CI find the problems.
3. If a check tool is only loosely version-pinned in the project (e.g.
   `ruff>=0.6` with no upper bound), be aware the CI-installed version can
   differ from the locally-installed one and silently disagree — see the
   diagnostic note below.

### After pushing

1. Get the run for this push: `gh run list --limit 1` (or filter by branch/
   workflow if there could be ambiguity with concurrent pushes).
2. Watch it to completion: `gh run watch <run-id> --exit-status`. Do not
   poll manually or guess — wait for the real result.
3. Confirm **every** job in the run succeeded, not just the one you expect
   to be affected by your change (e.g. a docs-only change can still break a
   job you didn't think to check).
4. If any job is red, treat the task as still in progress: read the failure
   log (`gh run view <run-id> --log-failed`), fix the root cause, commit,
   push, and repeat step 1–3. Only report the task complete once a run
   triggered by the current HEAD is fully green.

### Diagnostic tip: "local green, CI red, and the failure is unrelated to my diff"

This pattern usually means environment/dependency drift, not a bug in the
change just made. Before searching further:

- Compare the locally installed version of the failing tool against what
  the project's dependency file would resolve to fresh (e.g. `ruff --version`
  locally vs. the version range in `pyproject.toml`/`package.json`). CI
  typically does a clean install every run and will pick up new releases of
  loosely-pinned dev dependencies that a local environment — often installed
  once, months ago — never picked up.
- If that's the cause, the durable fix is to pin the dependency to a bounded
  range (not just a floor) in the project's manifest, verify locally with
  that exact version, and only then commit the pin change alongside any
  config adjustments the new version requires.

### Environment note: `gh` unavailable in WSL

If `gh` is not found in your shell but works on Windows (e.g. WSL with the
Windows GitHub CLI installed), don't treat it as a missing credential and
stop to ask the user. Create a symlink into a PATH directory — the Windows
keyring auth is shared, no re-login needed:

```bash
ln -s "/mnt/c/Program Files/GitHub CLI/gh.exe" /mnt/c/Users/<you>/.local/bin/gh
```

Verify once with `gh run list --limit 1`. If the Windows GitHub CLI is not
installed at all, then (and only then) credential provisioning is a real
user-gated step — surface it once with the exact install command instead of
re-prompting per command.

name: CI

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
  pull_request:

# 同一分支的新提交取消仍在跑的旧任务，避免排队浪费。
concurrency:
  group: ${{ github.workflow }}-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

# 供应链加固：本工作流只读仓库，不需要写权限。
permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  # 步骤清单的唯一真源是 tools/verify.py：CI 只调用它，不复制各步骤
  # （生命周期 / 文档链接 / 依赖 pin / pytest / ruff 全部由 verify.py 提供）。
  verify:
    name: verify (py${{ matrix.python-version }})
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        # 下限锁 requires-python；上限跟随，用于尽早发现新版本行为差异。
        python-version: ["3.11", "3.13"]
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v7
        with:
          python-version: ${{ matrix.python-version }}
      - run: pip install -e ".[dev]"
      - name: 统一验证（verify.py 全部步骤）
        run: python3 tools/verify.py
CLAUDE.md:34:- 测试/质量：pytest + ruff（check + format），唯一入口 `python3 tools/verify.py`
CLAUDE.md:42:- 质量门禁：统一入口 `python3 tools/verify.py`（串联文档门禁与测试；技术栈落地后扩展 lint/
.github/workflows/ci.yml:18:  # 步骤清单的唯一真源是 tools/verify.py：CI 只调用它，不复制各步骤
.github/workflows/ci.yml:35:        run: python3 tools/verify.py
